Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the networks space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 640 and the convergence rate is O(155/n^3).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by networks generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 640, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 155. QED.
